### Decisions taken in B3 (D-77)

None of these opens a question. Each is the most reasonable default within the author's remit; the supervisor may ratify or replace them through appended items (D-70).

| Id | Decision | Rationale |
|---|---|---|
| BS3-D-01 | The four phases are written in one file, as the design brief requires, instead of the four files of PHASES §0 and §2. Each phase keeps the heading shape `## <nn> <code> <phase name>`, its preamble and its GATE item, so assembly stays mechanical | Orchestrator brief; PHASES §0 assembly rule |
| BS3-D-02 | T-IMP-05 `exception_item` is created by CTR-5, the first item that raises exception items (05 RCP-20 quarantine), instead of by DIN; CTR-8 raises `COMBINATION_SUGGESTED` items and CTR-9 blocks activation on open items. Its foreign keys to `import_upload`, `import_row`, `sync_run` and `source_record` are added by the DIN revisions that create those tables (DG-MIG-03). GATE-DIN counts T-IMP-05 as created by CTR-5 | CTR requirements need the queue before DIN exists: REQ-REF-014 "goes to the exception queue", REQ-CON-010 suggestions stored as `COMBINATION_SUGGESTED` items (04 §16.14), and the activation checklist item `COMBINATION_SUGGESTIONS` (04 table 15.4-I). Without the table CTR cannot activate a contract |
| BS3-D-03 | T-INT-03 `outbox_message` is not created by DIN. PLF creates it because DG §5.11 DG-KRN-EVT-04 and the 04 §14.3 invitation row need it; DIN-12 adds only the `SYNC_REQUEST` topic handler and GATE-DIN counts the table as present | PHASES §1 PLF exit criteria (1) and (4) require the outbox before DIN; a second creation would break `make revision` |
| BS3-D-04 | Routes of a resource row whose table belongs to a later phase are built with the table: `GET, POST /policy-overrides`, `GET /policy-overrides/{id}` and `POST /policy-overrides/{id}/submit` (API-R-13 over T-CON-23) in CTR-15; `GET /contracts/{id}/sources` (API-R-28 over T-CON-02) in DIN-4. `GET /explain/report-runs/{id}/cell` (API-R-49 over T-RPT-02) is not built in CTR; the RPS item that creates `report_run` builds it | XR-19: no item depends on a later item |
| BS3-D-05 | RFD-5 creates `erev_api.domain.policies.simulation` with a provider registry. With no provider registered, the simulation report states "No contracts affected" (PRD BR-POL-01), which is exact while no contract table exists. CTR-16 registers the contract provider and the `POLICY_SIMULATION` job and re-runs the RFD-5 submission test with a real contract (header XR-12) | Configuration must be submittable in RFD; REQ-POL-006 is a CTR requirement |
| BS3-D-06 | REQ-SSP-006 completes in RFD-14 on resolution evidence: inception date, modification date and named version under the preset. CTR-15 contributes the override request, its approval and the override register, and tags CTL-010 for the override path | PHASES §6.6 assigns REQ-SSP-006 to RFD; the `SSP_OVERRIDE` subject is a CTR registry row (PHASES §5.3) |
| BS3-D-07 | REQ-REF-006 completes in RFD-3 with manual entry and approval; DIN-9 contributes the `fx_rates` CSV channel. REQ-CST-001 completes in CTR-5 through the API; DIN-9 contributes the `cost_events` CSV channel. REQ-DAT-012 completes in CTR-18, the last CTR item that adds a command for an importable object. REQ-REF-001 completes in RFD-2; CTR-3 adds the DB-05 freeze trigger that references `subledger_line` and re-runs the freeze test | PHASES §6 membership with the tables of PHASES §5.1 |
| BS3-D-08 | Captures whose SCREENS_B §15 state needs a journey of a later item capture a state built in the `screens.spec.ts` `beforeAll` through `createTenant` (DG-E2E-12) and API commands, until the journey item updates the row (BS-D-09): RFD-19 captures SF-15:setup in tenant `setup-capture` with checklist items 1 and 2 passed; LMG-8 and LMG-9 capture SF-19 in tenant `corrie-bay-capture`; LMG-10 and LMG-11 move the rows to WLD-T-21 and WLD-T-23 | BS-D-09 without forward dependencies |
| BS3-D-09 | Before CLO, the Avenmoor seed builders of RFD-16 and CTR-20 leave every period from Jan 2026 to Sep 2026 `open` for every entity and book; the CLO close-run builder applies WLD-P-02. Seed tests of this file assert only figures that no close run changes | XR-09; close runs are CLO capabilities |
| BS3-D-10 | Industry policy templates (REQ-POL-009) are created by the domain command `erev_api.domain.policies.industry.create_industry_templates(uow, cluster)` as DRAFT registry versions and DRAFT POB template versions; no route is added (04 API-R-13 has none) and the seed calls the command for WLD-T-02 to WLD-T-07 | PRD BR-POL-03 |
| BS3-D-11 | The DG-AK-45 helper `erev_api.domain.ssp.range_validation.validate_ranges` is built in RFD-13, which also makes `run_engine` evaluate `population` rows through it without changing the `run_engine` signature. The key `SSP-CHK-031-RANGE-VALIDATION-AT-PUBLICATION` still closes in AKS (PHASES §8.2.1); RFD-13 proves CHK-031 with unit tests | PHASES §8.3 COV-C-13; the key also asserts a booked licence, which needs the engine through stage 14 |
| BS3-D-12 | TC hints shared by a CTR and a DIN requirement are proved twice with the BS-D-21 prefix: `test_tc_setup_20_unmapped_product_blocks_activation` (CTR-9, REQ-REF-014) and `test_tc_setup_20_unmapped_product_row_message` (DIN-7, REQ-DAT-006). TC-delivery-17 E09 (backdated into a closed period) follows D-19 (warning `LATE_EVENT`, posting in the first open period with an origin period), not the background legacy text "closed period rejected"; DIN-8 proves E05 and E08 | D-19 outranks legacy port notes (D-§0) |
| BS3-D-13 | Answer-key hints of CTR requirements whose keys closed in AKS are re-run by the completing item with `make answer-keys ID=…` (DG-DONE-01). Keys of PHASES §8.2.2 and §8.2.3 are never named before EDS and PRP | PHASES §15.1 AK row |
| BS3-D-14 | Report definitions `migration_reconciliation` (RPT-41) and `parallel_run_comparison` (RPT-42) are seeded as global `report_definition` rows by the LMG-3 and LMG-5 revisions (DG-MIG-07) and run on the RPS report framework unchanged (BS-D-06) | Migrations seed global catalogues only |
| BS3-D-15 | The historical SSP calculator of RFD-15 reads an uploaded pool file of standalone sales (PRD BR-SSP-04; SCREENS §11.5 source `source_order_lines`). Each pool row carries `order_line_external_id`, and an exclusion stores `source_ref_type = 'source_order_line'` with `source_ref_id = uuid5(NAMESPACE_URL, "https://erev.dev/ns/ssp-pool/<pool file sha256>/<order_line_external_id>")`. The source `committed_obligations` returns no observations until CTR-2 registers the committed-obligation provider | T-REF-34 requires a uuid reference; the source-store tables are DIN and the obligation tables CTR |
| BS3-D-16 | Week-based calendars (E-50 `P445`, `P454`, `P544`, `P13`, `W5253`): the fiscal year ends on the `week_end_day` weekday that is last in the month before `fiscal_year_start_month` (`LAST_WEEKDAY_OF_MONTH`) or nearest to that month's last day (`NEAREST_WEEKDAY_TO_MONTH_END`); a 53-week year adds the extra week to the last period | 04 T-REF-04 names the anchors but not the generation rule |
| BS3-D-17 | The `POLICY_SIMULATION` job handler is registered by RFD-11, because `POST /policies/{id}/test` returns a job (04 §16.5), and it runs the RFD-5 provider registry; CTR-16 registers the contract provider. RFD-11 removes `POLICY_SIMULATION` from the DG-ARC-08 `PENDING_` tuple of job handlers, although PHASES §5.3 lists the kind under CTR | A route may not return a job kind without a handler (DG-KRN-JOB-01) |
| BS3-D-18 | PRD §2.12 fixture files that the seed and the tests both use are produced by deterministic generator functions in `backend/erev_api/domain/demo/fixtures.py` (for example `standalone_sales_pool()` for WLD-F-18), not stored as committed binaries. Legacy workbooks and databases stay under `backend/tests/fixtures/` (DG-PAR-02) | WLD-R-01 determinism; one source for seed and tests |
| BS3-D-19 | Until CTR-9 builds the activation command, the test factory `backend/tests/support/factories.py::activated_contract` activates a booked contract by appending `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED` through `events.stream.append_events` as the SYSTEM principal; CTR-9 replaces the body with the activation command and re-runs every test that uses the factory | CTR-3 to CTR-8 need active contracts to prove postings (ENGINE_SPEC S02-R-02) |
| BS3-D-20 | Tests of late events before CLO set a period to `closed` in a `pg`-marked test through `backend/tests/support/rows.py` (`period_state` update with its `period_state_transition` row in one transaction), which DG-TST-16 allows for the pg suite; the `period_lock_id` column carries a generated uuid because its foreign key is added by the CLO revision that creates `period_lock` | CTL-017 is a CTR control while period locks are CLO |
| BS3-D-21 | The committed-obligation provider of the SSP calculator (CTR-2) counts as a standalone sale every obligation version of an `ACTIVE` contract that has exactly one obligation and whose inception date lies in the run's date range; the unit price is the stated price divided by the quantity | 04 T-REF-32 names the source `committed_obligations` without a selection rule |
| BS3-D-22 | Route rows without a SCREENS §0.12 audit row are still captured by the item that builds them: `sf-03-edit` (RT-19, a DRAFT contract) in CTR-24 and `sf-07-detail` (RT-21, the seeded modification `CR-PELLWORTH-2026-07`; rev 1.8: until the demo seed holds it, the K-02 modification the row submits) in CTR-27 | PHASES §10 requires every route row to be cited by an item |
| BS3-D-23 | A `legacy_contract_setup` import satisfies the activation checklist of each contract as follows: `document_ref` = `import:<import_no>:<original file name>`; one `POB_DISTINCT_OVERRIDE` judgement per obligation whose `distinctness` is the template value `Distinct` or `Nondistinct`; and, per enabled book, a `COLLECTIBILITY` judgement with conclusion "Collectibility probable: legacy template import <import_no>" and a `COLLECTIBILITY_ASSESSED {is_probable: true}` event. The uploader prepares these records; the import approval reviews them in the approval transaction, so the reviewer differs from the preparer (DB-10) | PRD J-01.8 ("activation checklist passes for both") and BR-DAT-06 ("the import approval is the activation approval") with the table 15.4-I items `SOURCE_REFERENCE`, `DISTINCT_REVIEW` and `STEP1_RECORD` |
| BS3-D-24 | The adapter contract-test suite (05 ADP-15) is created by DIN-13 over the inbound adapters `SALESFORCE` and `STRIPE`; CLO adds the outbound GL adapters to the same parametrised suite. REQ-INT-002 completes in DIN-13 | The GL adapters are CLO capabilities (PHASES §5.2 mock routes) |
| BS3-D-25 | Onboarding from other systems (REQ-MIG-009) and acquired contracts (REQ-MIG-008) use the CSV v2 `contracts` template with the batch parameters `onboarding.method` (POL-210 literal), `cutover_date`, `onboarding_reason` (`SYSTEM_ONBOARDING` or `BUSINESS_COMBINATION`) and, for business combinations, `bc.expedient_modification_aggregation` and `bc.expedient_ssp_at_acquisition` (POL-216, POL-217). The template gains optional columns `opening.<member>` for each member of `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED.obligations[]` (04 §16.3) except `obligation_key`, plus `opening.fair_value_contract_liability`; the values apply to the obligation built from the row's line, and a line that expands into several obligations while carrying `opening.` values is refused at validation with a finding naming the column. These columns are the one documented exception to the NC-19 header rule; API-S-ContractCreate is unchanged because 04 E-03 lets only an import commit append `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED`. On approval the commit appends `CONTRACT_BOOKED`, `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED` and `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED`, and the activation checklist is satisfied as in BS3-D-23. No template code is added | 04 E-03 approval `IMPORT_COMMIT`; POLICIES POL-210 scope "import batch"; ENGINE_SPEC S07-R-13; 04 T-IMP-01 seeds no opening-balance template |
| BS3-D-26 | Mode (a) opening balances stage their work: `POST /migrations/{id}/import` writes `migrated_legacy_row` rows and computes, in a dry run, the per-contract events and the reconciliation lines without creating contracts; the `MIGRATION_PROMOTION` approval appends `CONTRACT_BOOKED`, `CONTRACT_ACTIVATED` and `OPENING_BALANCE_ESTABLISHED` in one transaction and publishes the preset version when the tenant policy is not already the preset. The promotion approval stands in for `IMPORT_COMMIT` because mode (a) has no import upload (T-MIG-01 `import_upload_ids` is for `REPLAY`); the activation checklist is satisfied as in BS3-D-23 with `document_ref = migration:<migration_no>`, the promotion approver reviewing the judgement records | PRD J-20.3 "staging load" and J-20.4 "contracts active" after promotion; CTL-048 reconciliation before promotion |

      - `backend/tests/domain/policies/test_rule_evaluation.py::test_most_specific_rule_wins_then_priority`: `POB_ASSIGNMENT` rules `product eq AVM-GW` and `product eq AVM-GW and region eq EU`: `/evaluate {product: "AVM-GW", region: "EU"}` returns the second rule; with equal specificity and priorities 10 and 20 the priority-20 rule wins

Rev 1.3 (supervisor ruling R-26 (b); 04 §16.10 rev 1.104): the vehicle was rule `AUTO-FX-01` on an FX rate set version that a user submitted; a rule approves no configuration version and nothing a person prepared, so the mechanism is witnessed on a system-originated standard item
      - `backend/tests/domain/policies/test_rule_floor.py` (rev 1.4; supervisor ruling R-26 on the security review's finding SN-9; 04 T-REF-26 and §16.10 rev 1.104): `::test_unconditional_approval_rule_is_refused_at_authoring` and `::test_auto_approval_rule_names_allow_listed_subject_types` — an `APPROVAL_ROUTING` or `AUTO_APPROVAL` rule without a condition, and an `AUTO_APPROVAL` rule that names no subject type or one outside the allow-list, return 422 `validation-failed` (rule `T-REF-26` on `conditions`); `::test_routing_rule_below_the_subjects_own_steps_is_refused_at_authoring` — `PERIOD_REOPEN` routed to one `judgement.review` step returns 422 on `outputs.steps`; `::test_publication_refuses_stored_rules_that_authoring_refuses` — the publication check reports the same findings over stored rules; `::test_second_step_and_its_role_survive_a_one_step_rule` and `::test_rule_that_adds_a_step_is_applied_as_written` — the routed steps are raised to the subject's own steps and a rule that adds a step routes as written; `::test_sn9_author_cannot_publish_routing_alone` — the reviewer's scenario end to end
      - `backend/tests/api/test_config_test_cases_api.py::test_config_test_cases_follow_subject_status`: `POST /config-test-cases {subject_type: "rule_set_version"}` succeeds while the version is DRAFT and returns 409 `configuration-frozen` once it is SUBMITTED (04 API-R-57)
      - Item CFG-BACKDATE-1 (fragment rev 1.10 / BUILD_SPEC 1.91; supervisor rulings R-112 (i) and R-113 (b) of 2026-09-30; PRD ERR-75; 04 §16.5 rev 1.160; 03 REQ-POL-007): a version that supersedes a published one never takes effect where a computation or an act has already chosen. The date form — `backend/tests/domain/policies/test_templates.py::test_err_75_a_superseding_template_version_is_dated_after_today`: version 2 of `TPL-SUB-DAILY` without a date, dated 1 July and dated 12 September (the day of the frozen clock) is refused at `/submit` with 422 `validation-failed`, field `effective_from`, rule `REQ-POL-007`; dated 13 September it is published, version 1 ends on that day and a contract dated 12 September resolves version 1; `::test_err_75_the_decision_checks_the_date_again_and_the_request_stays_pending`: submitted at 23:58 on 12 September for 13 September and decided two minutes later, the approval answers 422, no decision is stored, the request is PENDING and version 1 stays PUBLISHED; `backend/tests/domain/policies/test_lifecycle.py::test_err_75_an_assignment_rule_set_is_dated_after_today_in_every_entity`: a `POB_ASSIGNMENT` version 2 without a date or dated today is refused, and with an active entity in Pacific/Kiritimati so is 13 September; 14 September is published
      - Item CFG-BACKDATE-1 (fragment rev 1.10 / BUILD_SPEC 1.91; supervisor rulings R-112 (i) and R-113 (b) of 2026-09-30; PRD ERR-75; 04 §16.5 rev 1.160; 03 REQ-POL-007): a version that supersedes a published one never takes effect where a computation or an act has already chosen. The instant form — `backend/tests/domain/policies/test_lifecycle.py::test_err_75_a_rule_set_read_at_an_instant_never_takes_effect_in_the_past`: an `APPROVAL_ROUTING` version 2 dated one second before the clock is refused at `/submit`; without a date it is published and version 1 ends at the publication instant; `::test_err_75_the_decision_checks_the_instant_again_and_the_request_stays_pending`: dated one minute ahead and decided two minutes later, the approval answers 422 and the request stays PENDING until it is rejected; `backend/tests/domain/policies/test_registry_versions.py::test_err_75_a_superseding_policy_version_never_takes_effect_in_the_past`: version 2 of AVM-DE's practical expedients (`sfc.one_year_expedient`) dated between the publication of version 1 and the check is refused; dated at the instant of its submission it is published, and every earlier `known_at` keeps the answer version 1 gave; `::test_err_75_a_policy_decision_after_the_effective_time_leaves_the_request_pending`: decided two minutes after an effective time one minute ahead, the approval answers 422, the request stays PENDING and the preparer withdraws the version
      - Item CFG-BACKDATE-1 (fragment rev 1.10 / BUILD_SPEC 1.91; supervisor rulings R-112 (i) and R-113 (b) of 2026-09-30; PRD ERR-75; 04 §16.5 rev 1.160; 03 REQ-POL-007): a version that supersedes a published one never takes effect where a computation or an act has already chosen. The contract — `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_template_versions.py::test_err_75_a_later_template_version_never_reaches_a_posted_contract` (CTL-031): the monthly-even version 2 of `TPL-SUB-DAILY` dated on K-02's inception is refused; dated 1 October it is published, K-02 recomputes without an intent on version 1 (10,191.78 / 9,205.48 / 10,191.78) and a contract dated 1 October takes 10,000.00 a month; `::test_a_first_version_reaches_a_contract_only_through_its_product_and_the_pin_holds_it`: `TPL-SUB-MONTHLY` version 1, published in September with effect from 1 January and made the seat product's default template, leaves K-02 on the template its pin names. Moved under the rule (stale expectations, supervisor ruling R-113 (b)): `test_templates.py::test_new_version_supersedes_and_convention_reaches_drafts` dates its version 2 on 1 October where it was 1 July, and `backend/tests/pg/test_snapshot_retention_evidence.py::test_auto_approval_rule_is_withheld_and_an_auto_approved_version_refuses` decides before the effective instant of its superseding version

`::test_a_predecessor_that_moves_after_the_submit_voids_the_request` and `::test_the_approval_hook_checks_the_basis_under_its_locks` (409 `stale-approval`, the request `VOIDED` with `STALE_SUBJECT`, before the decision's locks and under the hook's), `::test_err_80_a_version_never_takes_effect_at_or_before_the_published_one`, `::test_a_legacy_parity_tenant_stays_one_through_later_versions`, `::test_the_legacy_parity_preset_is_a_whole_set`, `::test_a_patch_that_states_the_whole_set_carries_no_preset` and `::test_a_draft_that_keeps_the_presets_values_carries_the_preset` (after the review: the preset code follows what the statement keeps — a workspace never holds a preset's values under no preset), `::test_a_version_that_changes_the_retention_families_is_withheld_from_auto_approval` (D-98 candidate 86 over the difference, the supervisor's ruling on the review: a version that states the families, changes one or returns them to the default waits for a named human approver; one that only carries them forward does not) and `::test_the_retention_confirmation_is_the_approval_of_the_version_that_stated_the_families` (the snapshot evidence module's `test_a_later_platform_version_carries_the_families_and_waits_for_a_human` holds the carrying version on the committed database: stored as the whole set, its request pending without a decision, since no tenant rule approves a registry version) with `::test_err_77_names_the_retention_confirmation_and_its_start` (PRD ERR-77 is worded from the confirming version), and `::test_reopening_a_rejected_version_waits_for_a_create_in_its_scope_key` (two sessions; PRD SM-04) with `::test_a_rule_set_version_create_waits_for_the_row_a_reopening_holds` and the unit pins of `backend/tests/unit/test_config_version_reopen_lock.py`: `lifecycle.reopen` takes a kind's lock before it looks for an open version, and each of the five configuration version kinds supplies the lock its create takes, or none where its create locks the version rows; `registry.versions.published_of_key` reads the PUBLISHED version of a key `FOR UPDATE` when the approval hook asks; a kind's own refusal of a reopening is raised under the lock and before the look and asked again after it, and a registry create claims its key before it reads a predecessor;       - `backend/tests/unit/ssp/test_range_validation.py::test_coverage_unknown_outside_parity`: a range entry without a population gives `COVERAGE_UNKNOWN` under the default POL-073 and no finding under `NOT_ENFORCED`
      - `backend/tests/unit/answer_keys/test_runner_range_validation.py::test_run_engine_population_findings`: for the `world` of `SSP-CHK-031-RANGE-VALIDATION-AT-PUBLICATION`, the runner's population evaluation yields exactly `{code: RANGE_TOO_WIDE, severity: WARNING, subject: "ssp:SSP-MAIN:2026-07-01:LIC-31"}` and `{code: COVERAGE_TOO_LOW, severity: WARNING, subject: "ssp:SSP-MAIN:2026-07-01:LIC-31"}` without calling `compute` (DG §9.5.6 `exceptions.subject`)
      - `backend/tests/domain/ssp/test_publication.py::test_submit_requires_study_and_methodology`: `/submit` without an attachment of purpose `SSP_STUDY` returns 422 `ssp-study-required` with detail "Attach the SSP study before submitting this version."; with the study and `methodology_label` "Observable standalone sales, Jan-Aug 2026" the version is SUBMITTED with `approval_request_id` (REQ-SSP-008; PRD ERR-10)
      - `backend/tests/domain/ssp/test_publication.py::test_second_approver_when_mid_changes_above_threshold`: version `2026-H2` changing AVM-PLAT-100 mid from 100,000.00 to 112,000.00 (12.0%, above `approval.ssp_second_approver_threshold_ratio` 0.10) creates two `ssp.approve` steps; after `priya` approves step 1 the version stays SUBMITTED until `marcus` approves step 2 (REQ-SSP-007; PRD J-02.5 to J-02.7)
      - `backend/tests/domain/ssp/test_publication.py::test_single_approver_below_threshold`: a 5% mid change creates one step
      - `backend/tests/domain/ssp/test_publication.py::test_methodology_change_forces_second_approver`: `is_methodology_change = true` creates two steps

      - `backend/tests/domain/ssp/test_calculator.py::test_publication_coverage_counts_linked_run`: after the exclusion, the draft `2026-H2` created from the run, with AVM-PLAT-100 set to low 95,200.00, mid 112,000.00 and high 128,800.00 and its study attached, is submitted. It yields `COVERAGE_TOO_LOW` (`WARNING` under POL-073 `mode = WARN`) with coverage 16 of 39 (41.0%), counted over the non-excluded observations of the linked run inside the published range (POLICIES §3.3; PRD J-02.4, J-02.5 rev 1.3)

      - Rev 1.50 (BUILD_SPEC header 1.226; item SSP-ENTITY-SCOPE-1; 04 rev 1.277), `backend/tests/domain/ssp/test_calculator_reach.py::test_ssp_entity_scope_1_a_run_reads_within_its_requesters_entities`: the run of an SSP Analyst of AVM-DE alone over committed contract lines reads the order of AVM-DE and not the order of AVM-US, with no entity filter and with a filter that names AVM-US or an entity nobody knows, and stores her scope; a member of all entities reads both orders and stores a run of every entity, and her run filtered to AVM-DE stores that entity alone; the scope is the requester's `contract.read` and not the route's permission, a requester who reads no contract starts no run (403, with its DENIED event), a run names a book its requester may prepare, and the draft version needs the run and its book — a reader of the run whose `ssp.create` does not cover the run's book is answered 404. `::test_ssp_entity_scope_1_a_run_is_read_and_commanded_within_its_scope`: a run over AVM-US and a run of every entity are not listed to the member of AVM-DE, and the run, its results, its observations, an exclusion and the draft version answer her 404 as an id that names none; the member of AVM-US reads the run of AVM-US and makes the draft version from it; a run of AVM-DE alone is read by the member of AVM-DE; the reader is held to `contract.read` under the route's `ssp.read`. `backend/tests/unit/ssp/test_run_scope_snapshot.py::test_ssp_entity_scope_1_a_runs_scope_is_never_shortened_by_a_sandbox_copy`: a run's scope that loses an entity to a sandbox copy's cut is exported as a run of every entity, never as the remaining subset.
      - Rev 1.50 (BUILD_SPEC header 1.226; item SSP-CALC-LATEST-VERSION-1; 04 T-REF-34 rev 1.277), `backend/tests/domain/ssp/test_calculator_latest_version.py::test_ssp_calc_latest_version_1_a_combined_contract_is_observed_in_its_joint_group`: an order computed twice on its own and then combined has two versions in the group it left and one in the joint group; the calculator observes it, and the order it was combined with, in the joint group's version — the version of the computation that last held the obligation — and an order that was never combined in the latest version of its own group.

      - `backend/tests/domain/policies/test_judgements.py::test_submit_routes_judgement_review`: `POST /judgements/{id}/submit` creates a `JUDGEMENT_RECORD` request to `judgement.review`; approval sets `REVIEWED` with `reviewer_id` and `reviewed_at` (PRD §2.5; SM-10)
      - `backend/tests/domain/policies/test_judgements.py::test_jdg_discard_1_a_draft_judgement_record_is_discarded`: `POST /judgements/{id}/discard` moves a DRAFT record to `VOIDED` with one audit event — a draft that was never submitted, discarded by another holder of `judgement.create`, and one that was submitted, rejected and edited again, with its request's history; a SUBMITTED and a REJECTED record answer 409 `invalid-transition`; the discarded record takes no edit, submission or discard, keeps its number, and leaves the activation checklist, which a draft and a rejected record of the contract fail (rev 1.35; PRD SM-10; 04 T-CON-19, E-57, table 15.4-I rev 1.242)

- [ ] **CTR-8 Contract combination and combination suggestions.**
  - **Prerequisites:** CTR-7
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths: `backend/erev_api/domain/contracts/combination.py` (suggestion producer ENGINE_SPEC S02-R-14; group command); `backend/erev_api/approvals/subjects.py` (`COMBINATION_GROUP`); `backend/erev_api/api/v1/combinations.py`; `backend/erev_api/schemas/combinations.py`; `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_combination.py`; `backend/tests/architecture/test_registries_complete.py` (removes `COMBINATION_GROUP`)
    - Schema: none
    - API: API-R-28 `GET, POST /combination-groups`; `GET /combination-groups/{id}`; `POST /combination-groups/{id}/submit`, `/discard` (rev 1.61); `GET /combination-suggestions`; `POST /combination-suggestions/{id}/dismiss`
    - Engine: ENGINE_SPEC §2.3 S02-R-09, S02-R-10 consumed; no engine change
    - Screens: none
  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_combination.py::test_suggestion_for_related_party_within_window`: booking K-05 (C-05, inception 2026-09-03) after K-11 (C-11, inception 2026-09-01), both in group `HOLLENBRAND`, raises one `COMBINATION_SUGGESTED` item (severity `WARNING`, disposition `remediable`, `dedupe_key` `ENGINE:COMBINATION_SUGGESTED:<ascending contract ids>`); `GET /combination-suggestions?contract=<K-11 id>` returns it with `detection_window_days = 30` (REQ-CON-010; POL-016; 04 §16.14)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_combination.py::test_window_zero_disables_detection`: with `combination.detection_window_days = 0` no item is raised
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_combination.py::test_dismiss_requires_rationale`: a rationale shorter than 10 characters returns 422; "Separate purchasing entities; negotiated independently." sets the item `DISMISSED` with `resolution` equal to the rationale and audit event `exception_item.dismiss` (PRD §2.7 WLD-K-11)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_combination.py::test_combination_by_approved_command_reruns_group`: `POST /combination-groups {contract_ids: [A, B], criterion: "606-10-25-9(b)", rationale}` then `/submit`; approval by a `contract.approve` holder other than the preparer appends `COMBINATION_CHANGED` `JOIN` on both contracts, recomputes the group from its inception and posts differences in the first open period (REQ-CON-009; ENGINE_SPEC S02-R-09)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_combination.py::test_cross_currency_combination_rejected`: combining a USD and a EUR contract returns 422 `validation-failed` with `rule_id = "REQ-CON-019"` (REQ-CON-019)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_combination.py::test_uncombine_only_as_approved_error_correction`: `COMBINATION_CHANGED` `LEAVE` needs an approved `COMBINATION_GROUP` request with reason code `DATA_CORRECTION`; the member is recomputed in its own singleton group from its own inception (ENGINE_SPEC S02-R-09)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_combination.py::test_combine_empty_group_dirty_1_a_group_left_without_a_member_is_not_left_dirty`: after an approved join the two singleton groups hold no member and no `dirty_since`, and the combined group is computed and clean; after the last member has left, the combined group is cleared too and the contracts' own groups are computed (rev 1.47; 04 T-CON-03 rev 1.271; item COMBINE-EMPTY-GROUP-DIRTY-1)
      - `backend/tests/api/test_rows_without_entity_scope.py::test_a_combination_group_is_read_through_a_contract_the_session_reads` and `::test_a_group_of_two_entities_names_to_each_reader_the_members_it_reads`: a Revenue Accountant of AVM-DE alone is listed no group of US01 and answered 404 by id and for its `submit`; an order of AVM-DE and an order of AVM-AT are proposed for combination and combined — the reader of one order reads the group with that order in `member_contract_ids` and `member_count` 2 (before the approval: in `proposal.contract_ids`, `contract_count` 2), her `GET /contracts/{id}` states `combination_group` the same way, the filter `contract` finds nothing for the other order, and the `COMBINATION` record is not listed for her and 404 by id; a member of both entities reads both members and the record; a member of a third entity is listed no group (head R28-READS; 04 API-C-03 rev 1.275)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_combination_proposal_record.py` (seven tests) and `backend/tests/unit/test_combination_proposal_writer.py`: beside the proposal of a group a `COMBINATION` record of the same group stands in the table, as one made by hand before — the group is submitted with the record it names (`::test_a_record_at_rest_does_not_make_the_proposal_impossible_to_submit`), a pending request is approved and applied on what it showed (`::test_a_record_at_rest_does_not_change_what_a_pending_request_approves`), and a leave is answered and decided on the record its submission wrote (`::test_a_leave_is_decided_on_the_record_its_submission_wrote`); `POST /judgements` with topic `COMBINATION` for a combination group answers 422 on `topic` (`::test_a_combination_record_of_a_group_is_not_written_by_hand`), and `POST /judgements/{id}/submit` of the command's draft 409 (`::test_the_proposal_of_a_group_is_not_submitted_by_hand`); a `COMBINATION` record of a contract is written by hand and its audit event names its own contract, whatever its questionnaire lists (`::test_a_combination_record_of_a_contract_names_its_own_contract`, `::test_a_list_on_a_contracts_record_is_evidence`); the unit module pins the one caller that writes a group's record (item COMBINATION-PROPOSAL-RECORD-1; 04 T-CON-19 rev 1.283)

        - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_field_locks.py::test_attr_ssp_pin_1_the_events_route_refuses_a_version_pin`: a `LINE_ATTRIBUTES_CHANGED` whose `changes` name `ssp_book_version_id` or `ssp_version_label` returns 422 `validation-failed` with `rule_id = "REQ-SSP-006"` on `events.0.payload.changes.<member>` and the sentence that names the SSP override request, on the events route and on `/events/preview`; no submission and no request is written
        - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_field_locks.py::test_attr_ssp_pin_1_a_stored_version_pin_is_stale_at_its_approval`: an `ATTRIBUTE_CHANGE` submission that names a version, stored as the route stored it before the rule, is answered 409 `stale-approval` at its approval and pins nothing

      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_holds.py::test_hold_release_read_1_a_reader_of_another_entity_sees_no_hold`: a Revenue Accountant of another entity is answered 404 by the contract and its obligations, and the reader itself, asked in that scope, answers no hold of the contract although T-CON-20 shows its rows there (rev 1.67)

The after-posting pair is withdrawn from 1.0 until it is rebuilt (item REGROUP-AFTER-POSTING-AMOUNT-1)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_discard_1_any_draft_without_a_pending_request_is_discarded`: `POST /modifications/{id}/discard` moves a DRAFT to `VOIDED` with one audit event — a draft that was never submitted, and one that was submitted and withdrawn, discarded by another holder of `modification.create`; a SUBMITTED or VOIDED row answers 409 `invalid-transition` (`SM-03`); the contract's stream does not move; the discarded draft frees its reference, which a new draft then holds (rev 1.12; PRD SM-03; item MOD-DISCARD-1; the reference: rev 1.19, 04 rev 1.210, revision 0114)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_discard_1_waits_for_a_pending_review_of_the_modifications_own_record`: while the review of a `MODIFICATION_TREATMENT_OVERRIDE` record of the modification is pending the discard answers 409 naming the record (PRD ERR-82); after the review's preparer withdraws it the draft is discarded, and a record of the voided modification is not sent for review (rev 1.12)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_regroup.py::test_mod_discard_1_a_regroup_pair_is_discarded_whole`: discarding either row of the DRAFT pair of a regroup after posting voids both, each with its audit event, and appends no `REGROUPED` event (rev 1.12; rev 1.30: the pair is made as the route made it before the after-posting road was withdrawn)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_rejected_revise_1_a_rejected_modification_is_revised_and_approved`: `PATCH` of a REJECTED modification answers 200 and `DRAFT` with the proposal, the classification, the preview and the content hash reset and one audit event (`REJECTED` → `DRAFT`); the rejected request stays `REJECTED`; classified and previewed again it is submitted — a new request, the same reference — approved and `APPLIED` (rev 1.32; 04 §16.14 rev 1.236; PRD SM-03; item MOD-REJECTED-REVISE-1)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_rejected_revise_1_a_revised_draft_that_is_given_up_frees_its_reference`: a REJECTED modification holds its reference (422 for a second one) and is not discarded (409); revised it is a draft, discarded it is `VOIDED`, and the change order is entered again under the same reference; the contract's head does not move (rev 1.32)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_rejected_revise_1_the_rows_of_a_rejected_pair_return_to_draft_together`: the edit of either REJECTED row of a regroup pair returns both to `DRAFT`, each with its audit event, and the pair is then discarded whole (rev 1.32)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_patch_clear_1_a_nullable_member_sent_as_null_is_cleared`: `PATCH /modifications/{id}` leaves a member that is left out and clears `reference`, `price_change_amount`, `rationale` and `judgement_record_id` when they are sent as null; null for `lines` or `kind` leaves them; the cleared reference is free for the contract again (rev 1.12; 04 §16.14 rev 1.188; item MOD-PATCH-CLEAR-1)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_answer_preview_1_commands_answer_the_retained_preview`: after a preview the answers of `/submit`, `/withdraw` and `/discard` carry the `impact_preview` and `impact_summary.catch_up_total` that `GET` shows; `PATCH` and `/classify` clear the stored preview and answer null, and `GET` agrees (rev 1.12; item MOD-ANSWER-PREVIEW-1)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_prefill_read_1_a_read_answers_the_classification`: `GET /modifications/{id}` answers `prefill_reasons` as `/classify` last answered it and writes nothing; the submission keeps it and an edit clears it with the proposal; a price question the preparer answered, true or false, is named no more (rev 1.19; 04 T-CON-06 `classification`, §16.14 rev 1.210; item MOD-PREFILL-READ-1; the last clause: rev 1.31, 04 §16.14 rev 1.235, item MOD-PREFILL-STORED-ANSWER-1 — before, such a question was reported `attested` with the range, or as the engine computes it)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_prefill_stored_answer_1_a_stored_answer_is_not_named_a_proposal`: on K-02 the preparer's confirmation stores O2's `added_goods_distinct` true and `priced_at_ssp` false; the next `/classify` names neither and still proposes O1's question; a second `PATCH`, built from what no proposal names, keeps both; the classification behind it names no question and proposes the treatments of the first (rev 1.31; 04 §16.14 rev 1.235; item MOD-PREFILL-STORED-ANSWER-1)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_price_test_fact_1_the_price_test_is_answered_beside_the_proposals`: `price_tests` of a K-02 draft — `{}` before a classification and after an edit; while the price question is open its entry equals the proposal's (60,000 below 69,750 to 85,250, US-LIST@v1) and is not among the proposals; once both answers are stored nothing is proposed for O2 and the entry is still answered, by `/classify` and by `GET`; answered true it reads `value` true, reason `attested`, with the same price and range; O1, which the engine does not price-test, has no entry (rev 1.39; 04 §16.14 rev 1.250; item MOD-PRICE-TEST-FACT-1)
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_modifications_boundary.py::test_mod_price_test_fact_1_the_price_test_is_stated_whatever_the_preparer_answered`: `modifications.price_tests` over the engine's nodes — the entry of a computed test, of an attested one with a range, with a point and without an SSP, and no entry without a node (rev 1.39)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_classification_keys_1_an_obligation_keyed_like_a_member_keeps_its_proposals`: the K-02 upgrade with its added line keyed `proposal`, `price_tests` and `obligations` in turn — the row holds the three members and no other, `prefill_reasons` names the added obligation's two questions under its key and O1's under its own, `proposal_detail` is the engine's detail and `price_tests` the line's test; `GET` answers the same (rev 1.59; 04 T-CON-06 `classification`, §16.14 rev 1.286; item MOD-CLASSIFICATION-KEYS-1)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_classification_keys_1_a_classification_stored_flat_reads_as_none`: a classification written past the commands as it was stored before the item reads as none on `GET` — the three members `{}` — while the row's treatments, summary and basis stand; the next `/classify` writes the three members (rev 1.59)
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_modifications_boundary.py::test_mod_classification_keys_1_the_stored_classification_is_read_by_its_three_members`: `modifications.stored_classification` — obligations keyed like the members, and a classification that proposes and tests nothing (rev 1.59)
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_modifications_boundary.py::test_mod_classification_keys_1_a_classification_stored_flat_reads_as_none`: what is not the three members by name and shape reads as none — the flat document, the two collisions as they were stored, and the flat document whose only obligation was keyed `obligations` (rev 1.59)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_preview_journal_rule_1_the_lines_are_those_of_the_asking_day`: one K-02 draft previews Dr contract liability / Cr revenue 1,906.79 net in September and 16,039.25 once the period tick has opened FY2026-P10 — October's whole 14,132.46 beside it; the summary states `computed_at` and `computed_period_key`; the September preview is not stale and the submission takes it (rev 1.19; 04 API-S-ImpactSummary rev 1.210; item MOD-PREVIEW-JOURNAL-RULE-1)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_linked_estimates_1_a_version_is_created_inside_a_draft_modification`: `POST /estimates/{id}/versions` with `modification_id` links the version to a DRAFT modification of the estimate's contract — 422 for a modification of another contract or one that is not a draft; `GET /modifications/{id}` lists it in `linked_estimate_versions`; PATCH does not take the member (rev 1.19; 04 T-CON-13 `modification_id`; item MOD-LINKED-ESTIMATES-1)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_linked_estimates_1_the_versions_are_approved_before_the_modification`: `/submit` answers 409 naming a linked version that is not approved (PRD ERR-87); once the version is approved — its `ESTIMATE_CHANGED` moves the contract's head and the basis of the stored preview — the modification is previewed again, submitted and approved; an estimate version approved while a modification of its contract waits for approval makes that request stale, which is why the versions come first; a link written past the commands after the submission makes the request stale as well, and a discarded version does not count (rev 1.19; 04 §16.10, §16.14 rev 1.210)
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_modifications_boundary.py::test_mod_linked_estimates_1_the_approval_hook_refuses_as_the_backstop`: the approval's own check — after the locks and the fresh-basis check, one entry for every linked version that is not approved, ending on the approval — for a linked version handed to the hook; no command, DB-03 pair or request basis leads to that state (rev 1.19; PRD ERR-87)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_linked_estimates_1_a_discard_waits_for_a_submitted_version`: the discard answers 409 naming a linked version that is waiting for approval (PRD ERR-83); after its withdrawal the modification is discarded, the linked version stays as it is and its `submit` answers 409 naming the modification (PRD ERR-88) (rev 1.19)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_linked_judgements_1_the_records_are_reviewed_before_the_modification`: `/submit` answers 409 `invalid-transition` (`SM-03`) naming each judgement record of the modification that is a draft or waits for review, in number order; with one reviewed, one rejected and one discarded the modification is submitted; a record added since holds the approval by name until it is reviewed, and its review beside the waiting modification leaves the request fresh: approved and applied (rev 1.35; PRD ERR-95; 04 §16.14 rev 1.242; item MOD-LINKED-JUDGEMENTS-1)
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_modifications.py::test_mod_linked_judgements_1_a_departure_without_its_reviewed_record_still_answers_err_54`: the records are asked behind the row's own findings — a departure whose `MODIFICATION_TREATMENT_OVERRIDE` record is a draft answers 422 `REQ-MOD-002` (PRD ERR-54), not the 409 of ERR-95 (rev 1.35)
      - `backend/tests/architecture/test_copy_catalogue_drift.py::test_dg_arc_13_copy_equals_code_for_the_modification_judgement_records_row`: PRD ERR-95 equals `modifications.RECORD_NOT_REVIEWED` word for word, and the row quotes the ending of the approval's sentence (rev 1.35; DG-ARC-13)
      - `backend/tests/pg/test_migration_0114.py::test_0114_a_discarded_draft_frees_its_reference`: `ux_modification__reference` does not count a `VOIDED` row without an applied event and counts one with it (rev 1.19; 04 T-CON-06 keys rev 1.210)
      - `backend/tests/pg/test_migration_0114.py::test_0114_the_versions_of_a_modification_are_read_through_their_index`: as `erev_app` under the tenant's policy the read behind `linked_estimate_versions` enters `ix_estimate_version__modification` bound by the tenant and the modification (rev 1.19; 04 T-CON-13 keys rev 1.210)

      - `backend/tests/domain/imports/test_commit.py::test_quarantine_mode_commits_valid_rows`: with `data.quarantine_failed_rows = true`, a file of 3 rows with 1 `ERROR` row commits 2 rows after approval and leaves 1 `OPEN` exception item; with the default `false` the same file is `INVALID` (REQ-DAT-008; PRD BR-DAT-01)

      - Item IMPORT-HEADER-CELL-CONFLICT-1 (header rev 1.270, fragment rev 1.72; register index 295; 05 IPL-05 rev 1.210, 04 rev 1.310, PRD IMP-147 rev 1.204) — a cell the rows of one object repeat is stated once: `backend/tests/domain/imports/csv_v2/test_header_cell_conflict.py::test_a_mapping_version_is_not_stored_against_a_later_row_of_its_file`, `backend/tests/domain/imports/csv_v2/test_header_cell_conflict.py::test_an_equal_or_a_blank_later_cell_loads_as_before`, `backend/tests/domain/imports/csv_v2/test_header_cell_conflict.py::test_a_contract_is_not_booked_against_a_later_row_of_its_file`, `backend/tests/domain/imports/csv_v2/test_header_cell_conflict.py::test_an_estimate_is_not_stored_against_a_later_row_of_its_file`, `backend/tests/domain/imports/csv_v2/test_header_cell_conflict.py::test_a_document_is_not_stored_against_a_later_row_of_its_file`, `backend/tests/domain/imports/csv_v2/test_header_cell_conflict.py::test_the_rows_of_one_line_that_agree_make_one_line_with_their_tax_lines`, `backend/tests/domain/imports/csv_v2/test_header_cell_conflict.py::test_an_ssp_version_is_not_stored_against_a_later_row_of_its_file`, `backend/tests/unit/imports/test_repeated_cells.py::test_no_column_of_a_grouped_template_stands_outside_the_rule`, `backend/tests/unit/imports/test_repeated_cells.py::test_plans_group_by_the_declared_key`: a CSV v2 file one of whose later rows states another value than the first row of its object in a cell those rows repeat is refused on that row at that column (`HEADER_VALUE_CONFLICT`, PRD IMP-147), naming both rows and both values — a mapping version, a contract, an estimate, a document and its line, the version of an SSP book and its entry: outside quarantine mode the upload is INVALID and nothing is stored; in quarantine mode the refused row takes its unit with it — the mapping version, the contract or the SSP book stays behind whole — and another mapping version or contract of the file loads; an equal or a blank later cell loads as before, and two rows of one invoice line that state its amount as 54000.00 and as 54000 are one line with two tax lines; no column of a template that makes one object of several rows stands outside its key, its repeated cells and the cells a row states for itself, and `plans` groups by the declared key (before: the object was stored as its first row stated it and nothing was said of the later row)
